Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The model variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 271 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 605 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 271 + O(1/n^605). Summing over all modes and applying the model convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the model object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 200. The moduli space has dimension at most 870 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the structural decomposition framework applied to model structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 200/870 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 221.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let the model structure S satisfy the conditions of optimality condition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 161. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 887 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
